# Graph Report - devbible  (2026-08-28)

## Corpus Check
- 6431 files · ~6,825,212 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 37691 nodes · 51114 edges · 2147 communities (1434 shown, 713 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 733 edges (avg confidence: 0.82)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `90d9d8b1`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
